</div>
<div id="LC165" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft entered the operating system (OS) business in 1980</div>
<div id="LC166" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with its own version of Unix called Xenix,[18] but it was MS-DOS that solidified the company&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC167" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">dominance. IBM awarded a contract to Microsoft in November 1980 to provide a version of the CP/M</div>
<div id="LC168" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">OS to be used in the IBM Personal Computer (IBM PC).[19] For this deal, Microsoft purchased a CP/M</div>
<div id="LC169" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">clone called 86-DOS from Seattle Computer Products which it branded as MS-DOS, although IBM</div>
<div id="LC170" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rebranded it to IBM PC DOS. Microsoft retained ownership of MS-DOS following the release of the</div>
<div id="LC171" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">IBM PC in August 1981. IBM had copyrighted the IBM PC BIOS, so other companies had to reverse</div>
<div id="LC172" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">engineer it for non-IBM hardware to run as IBM PC compatibles, but no such restriction applied to the</div>
<div id="LC173" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">operating systems. Microsoft eventually became the leading PC operating systems vendor.[20][21]: 210  The</div>
<div id="LC174" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company expanded into new markets with the release of the Microsoft Mouse in 1983, as well as with a</div>
<div id="LC175" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">publishing division named Microsoft Press.[10]: 232  Paul Allen resigned from Microsoft in 1983 after</div>
<div id="LC176" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">developing Hodgkin&#39;s lymphoma.[22] Allen claimed in Idea Man: A Memoir by the co-founder of</div>
<div id="LC177" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft that Gates wanted to dilute his share in the company when he was diagnosed with Hodgkin&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC178" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">disease because he did not think that he was working hard enough.[23] Allen later invested in low-tech</div>
<div id="LC179" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">sectors, sports teams, commercial real estate, neuroscience, private space flight, and more.[24]</div>
<div id="LC180" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC181" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC182" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC183" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">1985–1994: Windows and Office</div>
<div id="LC184" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft released Windows 1.0 on November 20, 1985, as a</div>
<div id="LC185" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">graphical extension for MS-DOS,[10]: 242–243, 246  despite</div>
<div id="LC186" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">having begun jointly developing OS/2 with IBM that</div>
<div id="LC187" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">August.[25] Microsoft moved its headquarters from Bellevue</div>
<div id="LC188" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to Redmond, Washington, on February 26, 1986, and went</div>
<div id="LC189" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">public with an initial public offering (IPO) at the NASDAQ</div>
<div id="LC190" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">exchange on March 13,[26] with the resulting rise in stock</div>
<div id="LC191" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">making an estimated four billionaires and 12,000 millionaires</div>
<div id="LC192" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">from Microsoft employees.[27] Microsoft released its version</div>
<div id="LC193" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of OS/2 to original equipment manufacturers (OEMs) on Windows 1.0 was released on November</div>
<div id="LC194" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">April 2, 1987.[10] In 1990, the Federal Trade Commission 20, 1985, as the first version of the</div>
<div id="LC195" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">examined Microsoft for possible collusion due to the Windows line.</div>
<div id="LC196" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">partnership with IBM, marking the beginning of more than a</div>
<div id="LC197" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">decade of legal clashes with the government.[28] : 243–244 </div>
<div id="LC198" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC199" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Meanwhile, the company was at work on Microsoft Windows NT, which was heavily based on their copy</div>
<div id="LC200" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of the OS/2 code. It shipped on July 21, 1993, with a new modular kernel and the 32-bit Win32</div>
<div id="LC201" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">application programming interface (API), making it easier to port from 16-bit (MS-DOS-based)</div>
<div id="LC202" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Windows. Microsoft informed IBM of Windows NT, and the OS/2 partnership deteriorated.[29]</div>
<div id="LC203" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC204" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 1990, Microsoft introduced the Microsoft Office suite which bundled separate applications such as</div>
<div id="LC205" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft Word and Microsoft Excel.[10]: 301  On May 22, Microsoft launched Windows 3.0, featuring</div>
<div id="LC206" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">streamlined user interface graphics and improved protected mode capability for the Intel 386</div>
<div id="LC207" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">processor,[30] and both Office and Windows became dominant in their respective areas.[31][32]</div>
<div id="LC208" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC209" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 27, 1994, the Department of Justice&#39;s Antitrust Division filed a competitive impact statement that</div>
<div id="LC210" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">said: &quot;Beginning in 1988 and continuing until July 15, 1994, Microsoft induced many OEMs to execute</div>
<div id="LC211" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">anti-competitive per processor licenses. Under a per-processor license, an OEM pays Microsoft a royalty</div>
<div id="LC212" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for each computer it sells containing a particular microprocessor, whether the OEM sells the computer</div>
<div id="LC213" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with a Microsoft operating system or a non-Microsoft operating system. In effect, the royalty payment to</div>
<div id="LC214" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft when no Microsoft product is being used acts as a penalty, or tax, on the OEM&#39;s use of a</div>
<div id="LC215" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">competing PC operating system. Since 1988, Microsoft&#39;s use of per processor licenses has increased.&quot;[33]</div>
<div id="LC216" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC217" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">1995–2007: Foray into the Web, Windows 95, Windows XP, and Xbox</div>
<div id="LC218" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Following Bill Gates&#39;s internal &quot;Internet Tidal Wave memo&quot; on May 26, 1995, Microsoft began to</div>
<div id="LC219" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">redefine its offerings and expand its product line into computer networking and the World Wide Web.[34]</div>
<div id="LC220" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC221" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">With a few exceptions of new companies, like Netscape, Microsoft was the only major and established</div>
<div id="LC222" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company that acted fast enough to be a part of the World Wide Web practically from the start. Other</div>
<div id="LC223" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">companies like Borland, WordPerfect, Novell, IBM and Lotus, being much slower to adapt to the new</div>
<div id="LC224" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">situation, would give Microsoft market dominance.[35]</div>
<div id="LC225" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC226" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The company released Windows 95 on August 24, 1995, featuring pre-emptive multitasking, a completely</div>
<div id="LC227" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">new user interface with a novel start button, and 32-bit compatibility; similar to NT, it provided the</div>
<div id="LC228" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Win32 API.[36][37]: 20  Windows 95 came bundled with the online service MSN, which was at first</div>
<div id="LC229" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">intended to be a competitor to the Internet, and (for OEMs) Internet Explorer, a Web browser. Internet</div>
<div id="LC230" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Explorer was not bundled with the retail Windows 95 boxes, because the boxes were printed before the</div>
<div id="LC231" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC232" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC233" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC234" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">team finished the Web browser, and instead were included in</div>
<div id="LC235" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Windows 95 Plus! pack.[38] Backed by a high-profile</div>
<div id="LC236" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">marketing campaign[39] and what The New York Times called</div>
<div id="LC237" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;the splashiest, most frenzied, most expensive introduction of</div>
<div id="LC238" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a computer product in the industry&#39;s history,&quot;[40] Windows 95</div>
<div id="LC239" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">quickly became a success.[41] Branching out into new markets</div>
<div id="LC240" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in 1996, Microsoft and General Electric&#39;s NBC unit created a</div>
<div id="LC241" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">new 24/7 cable news channel, MSNBC.[42] Microsoft created</div>
<div id="LC242" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Windows CE 1.0, a new OS designed for devices with low</div>
<div id="LC243" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">memory and other constraints, such as personal digital</div>
<div id="LC244" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC245" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 1996, Microsoft released Windows CE,</div>
<div id="LC246" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a version of the operating system meant assistants.[43] In October 1997, the Justice Department filed a</div>
<div id="LC247" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for personal digital assistants and other motion in the Federal District Court, stating that Microsoft</div>
<div id="LC248" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">tiny computers, shown here on the HP violated an agreement signed in 1994 and asked the court to</div>
<div id="LC249" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">300LX. stop the bundling of Internet Explorer with</div>
<div id="LC250" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC251" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Windows.[10]: 323–324 </div>
<div id="LC252" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC253" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On January 13, 2000, Bill Gates handed over the CEO position to</div>
<div id="LC254" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Steve Ballmer, an old college friend of Gates and employee of the</div>
<div id="LC255" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company since 1980, while creating a new position for himself as</div>
<div id="LC256" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Chief Software Architect.[10]: 111, 228 [14] Various companies including</div>
<div id="LC257" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft formed the Trusted Computing Platform Alliance in</div>
<div id="LC258" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">October 1999 to (among other things) increase security and protect</div>
<div id="LC259" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">intellectual property through identifying changes in hardware and</div>
<div id="LC260" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">software. Critics decried the alliance as a way to enforce While primarily focused on</div>
<div id="LC261" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">indiscriminate restrictions over how consumers use software, and software, Microsoft also</div>
<div id="LC262" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">over how computers behave, and as a form of digital rights produced a number of</div>
<div id="LC263" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">management: for example, the scenario where a computer is not only successful PC accessories, such</div>
<div id="LC264" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC265" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">secured for its owner but also secured against its owner as as the IntelliMouse computer</div>
<div id="LC266" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC267" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">well.[44][45] On April 3, 2000, a judgment was handed down in the mice shown here</div>
<div id="LC268" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC269" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">case of United States v. Microsoft Corp.,[46] calling the</div>
<div id="LC270" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company an &quot;abusive monopoly&quot;.[47] Microsoft later settled</div>
<div id="LC271" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with the U.S. Department of Justice in 2004.[26]</div>
<div id="LC272" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC273" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On October 25, 2001, Microsoft released Windows XP,</div>
<div id="LC274" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">unifying the mainstream and NT lines of OS under the NT</div>
<div id="LC275" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">codebase.[48] The company released the Xbox later that year,</div>
<div id="LC276" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">entering the video game console market dominated by Sony Microsoft released the first installment in</div>
<div id="LC277" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and Nintendo.[49] In March 2004 the European Union brought the Xbox series of consoles in 2001. The</div>
<div id="LC278" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">antitrust legal action against the company, citing it abused its Xbox, graphically powerful compared to</div>

</div>
<div id="LC280" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">dominance with the Windows OS, resulting in a judgment of its rivals, featured a standard PC&#39;s</div>

<div id="LC283" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">€497  million ($613  million) and requiring Microsoft to</div>

<div id="LC288" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

<div id="LC291" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Increasingly present in the hardware business following Xbox, Microsoft 2006 released the Zune series of</div>
<div id="LC292" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">digital media players, a successor of its previous software platform Portable Media Center. These</div>
<div id="LC293" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">expanded on previous hardware commitments from Microsoft following its original Microsoft Mouse in</div>
<div id="LC294" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">1983; as of 2007 the company sold the best-selling wired keyboard (Natural Ergonomic Keyboard 4000),</div>
<div id="LC295" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">mouse (IntelliMouse), and desktop webcam (LifeCam) in the United States. That year the company also</div>
<div id="LC296" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">launched the Surface &quot;digital table&quot;, later renamed PixelSense.[53]</div>

</div>
<div id="LC298" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2007–2011: Microsoft Azure, Windows Vista, Windows 7, and Microsoft</div>

</div>

</div>

<div id="LC318" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">€899  million ($1.4  billion) for Microsoft&#39;s lack of compliance with the March 2004 judgment on</div>

<div id="LC324" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Gates retired from his role as Chief Software Architect on June 27, 2008, a decision announced in June</div>

<div id="LC330" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">public. Windows 7&#39;s focus was on refining Vista with ease-of-use features and performance</div>
<div id="LC331" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">enhancements, rather than an extensive reworking of Windows.[64][65][66]</div>
<div id="LC332" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC333" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">As the smartphone industry boomed in the late 2000s, Microsoft had struggled to keep up with its rivals</div>
<div id="LC334" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in providing a modern smartphone operating system, falling behind Apple and Google-sponsored Android</div>

<div id="LC336" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">system, Windows Mobile, replacing it with the new Windows Phone OS that was released in October that</div>

</div>
<div id="LC338" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC339" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC340" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">year.[68][69] It used a new user interface design language, codenamed &quot;Metro&quot;, which prominently used</div>
<div id="LC341" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">simple shapes, typography, and iconography, utilizing the concept of minimalism. Microsoft implemented</div>
<div id="LC342" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a new strategy for the software industry, providing a consistent user experience across all smartphones</div>
<div id="LC343" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">using the Windows Phone OS. It launched an alliance with Nokia in 2011 and Microsoft worked closely</div>
<div id="LC344" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">with the company to co-develop Windows Phone,[70] but remained partners with long-time Windows</div>
<div id="LC345" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Mobile OEM HTC.[71] Microsoft is a founding member of the Open Networking Foundation started on</div>
<div id="LC346" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">March 23, 2011. Fellow founders were Google, HPE Networking, Yahoo!, Verizon Communications,</div>
<div id="LC347" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Deutsche Telekom and 17 other companies. This nonprofit organization is focused on providing support</div>
<div id="LC348" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for a cloud computing initiative called Software-Defined Networking.[72] The initiative is meant to speed</div>

<div id="LC350" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">centers, and other networking areas.[73]</div>

<div id="LC369" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2012, Microsoft sold its 50% stake in MSNBC, which it had run as a joint venture with NBC</div>

<div id="LC384" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">dark due to an infrared sensor, higher-end processing power and new software, the ability to distinguish</div>
<div id="LC385" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">between fine movements (such as a thumb movement), and determining a user&#39;s heart rate by looking at</div>

</div>

</div>
<div id="LC388" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>

<div id="LC393" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC394" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 19, 2013, Microsoft stocks suffered its biggest one-</div>

</div>

<div id="LC401" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In line with the maturing PC business, in July 2013,</div>

<div id="LC403" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Operating systems, Apps, Cloud, and Devices. All previous divisions will be dissolved into new divisions</div>

<div id="LC407" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2014–2020: Windows 10, Microsoft Edge, and HoloLens</div>

<div id="LC411" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

<div id="LC412" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On the same day, John W. Thompson took on the role of</div>

<div id="LC419" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the video game development company Mojang, best known 2014.</div>

</div>
<div id="LC421" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for Minecraft, for $2.5  billion.[100] On June 8, 2017,</div>

<div id="LC423" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">$100 million.[101][102]</div>
<div id="LC424" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

<div id="LC572" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">connects students and tutors in numerous subjects. The acquisition positioned Microsoft to grow its</div>
<div id="LC573" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">presence in the market of providing online education to large numbers of people.[154] In the same month,</div>
<div id="LC574" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft acquired Australia-based video editing software company Clipchamp.[155]</div>
<div id="LC575" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC576" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In October 2021, Microsoft announced that it began rolling out end-to-end encryption (E2EE) support for</div>
<div id="LC577" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft Teams calls in order to secure business communication while using video conferencing</div>
<div id="LC578" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">software. Users can ensure that their calls are encrypted and can utilize a security code that both parties</div>
<div id="LC579" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">on a call must verify on their respective ends.[156] On October 7, Microsoft acquired Ally.io, a software</div>
<div id="LC580" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">service that measures companies&#39; progress against OKRs. Microsoft plans to incorporate Ally.io into its</div>
<div id="LC581" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Viva family of employee experience products.[157]</div>
<div id="LC582" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC583" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On January 18, 2022, Microsoft announced the acquisition of American video game developer and</div>
<div id="LC584" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">holding company Activision Blizzard in an all-cash deal worth $68.7 billion.[158] Activision Blizzard is</div>
<div id="LC585" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">best known for producing franchises, including but not limited to Warcraft, Diablo, Call of Duty,</div>
<div id="LC586" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">StarCraft, Candy Crush Saga, Crash Bandicoot, Spyro, Tony Hawk&#39;s, Guitar Hero, and Overwatch.[159]</div>
<div id="LC587" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC588" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Activision and Microsoft each released statements saying the acquisition was to benefit their businesses</div>
<div id="LC589" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in the metaverse, many saw Microsoft&#39;s acquisition of video game studios as an attempt to compete</div>
<div id="LC590" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">against Meta Platforms, with TheStreet referring to Microsoft wanting to become &quot;the Disney of the</div>
<div id="LC591" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">metaverse&quot;.[160][161] Microsoft also named Phil Spencer, head of the Xbox brand since 2014, the</div>
<div id="LC592" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">inaugural CEO of the newly established Microsoft Gaming division, which now houses the Xbox</div>
<div id="LC593" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC594" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC595" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC596" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">operations team and the three publishers in the company&#39;s portfolio (Xbox Game Studios, ZeniMax</div>
<div id="LC597" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Media, Activision Blizzard). Microsoft has not released statements regarding Activision&#39;s recent legal</div>
<div id="LC598" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">controversies regarding employee abuse, but reports have alleged that Activision CEO Bobby Kotick, a</div>
<div id="LC599" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">major target of the controversy, will leave the company after the acquisition is finalized.[162] The deal</div>
<div id="LC600" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">was closed on October 13, 2023.[163]</div>
<div id="LC601" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC602" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In December 2022, Microsoft announced a new 10-year deal with the London Stock Exchange Group for</div>
<div id="LC603" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">products including Microsoft Azure; Microsoft acquired around 4% of LSEG as part of the deal.[164]</div>
<div id="LC604" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC605" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In January 2023, CEO Satya Nadella announced Microsoft would lay off some 10,000 employees.[165]</div>
<div id="LC606" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC607" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The announcement came a day after hosting a Sting concert for 50 people, including Microsoft</div>
<div id="LC608" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">executives, in Davos, Switzerland.[166]</div>
<div id="LC609" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC610" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On January 23, 2023, Microsoft announced a new multi-year, multi-billion dollar investment deal with</div>
<div id="LC611" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ChatGPT developer OpenAI.[167]</div>
<div id="LC612" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC613" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In June 2023, Microsoft released Azure Quantum Elements to run molecular simulations and calculations</div>
<div id="LC614" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in computational chemistry and materials science using a combination of AI, high-performance</div>
<div id="LC615" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">computing and quantum computing.[168] The service includes Copilot, a GPT-4 based large language</div>
<div id="LC616" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">model tool to query and visualize data, write code, initiate simulations, and educate researchers.[168]</div>

</div>
<div id="LC618" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">At a November 2023 developer conference, Microsoft announced two new custom-designed computing</div>
<div id="LC619" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">chips: The Maia chip, designed to run large language models, and Cobalt CPU, designed to power general</div>
<div id="LC620" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cloud services on Azure.[169][170]</div>
<div id="LC621" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC622" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On November 20, 2023, Satya Nadella announced that Sam Altman, who had been ousted as CEO of</div>
<div id="LC623" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">OpenAI just days earlier, and Greg Brockman, who had resigned as president, would join Microsoft to</div>
<div id="LC624" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">lead a new advanced AI research team.[171][172] However, the plan was short-lived, as Altman was</div>
<div id="LC625" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">subsequently reinstated as OpenAI&#39;s CEO and Brockman rejoined the company amid pressure from</div>
<div id="LC626" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">OpenAI&#39;s employees and investors on its board.[173] In March 2024, Inflection AI&#39;s cofounders Mustafa</div>
<div id="LC627" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Suleyman and Karen Simonyan announced their departure from the company in order to start Microsoft</div>
<div id="LC628" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">AI, with Microsoft acqui-hiring nearly the entirety of its 70-person workforce. As part of the deal,</div>
<div id="LC629" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft paid Inflection $650 million to license its technology.[174][175]</div>

</div>
<div id="LC631" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In January 2024, Microsoft became the most valued publicly traded company. Meanwhile, that month, the</div>
<div id="LC632" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company announced a subscription offering of artificial intelligence for small businesses via Copilot</div>
<div id="LC633" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Pro.[176][177]</div>

</div>
<div id="LC635" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In April 2024, Microsoft made a $1.5 billion investment in the Emirati AI firm G42. As part of the deal,</div>
<div id="LC636" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">G42 said it would use the Microsoft Azure platform for its AI development and deployment.[178][179]</div>
<div id="LC637" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC638" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Later that month, Microsoft unveiled plans to invest $1.7 billion in developing AI and cloud</div>
<div id="LC639" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">infrastructure in Indonesia. The plan includes establishment of data centers and partnerships to support</div>
<div id="LC640" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">digital transformation efforts.[180]</div>
<div id="LC641" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC642" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In May 2024, Microsoft announced a $3.3 billion investment to build an artificial intelligence hub in</div>
<div id="LC643" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">southeast Wisconsin, tripling its initial proposal. This initiative, unveiled by President Joe Biden in</div>
<div id="LC644" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Racine County, includes constructing a data center, creating 2,300 construction jobs by 2025, and 2,000</div>
<div id="LC645" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">permanent jobs over time, alongside establishing an AI co-innovation lab at UW-Milwaukee to train up to</div>
<div id="LC646" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">1,000 individuals by 2030.[181]</div>

<div id="LC648" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

</div>
<div id="LC650" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In June 2024, Microsoft announced it would be laying off 1,000 employees from the company&#39;s mixed</div>
<div id="LC651" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">reality and Azure cloud computing divisions.[182][183]</div>
<div id="LC652" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC653" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In June 2024, Microsoft announced that it was building a &quot;hyperscale data centre&quot; in South East</div>
<div id="LC654" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Leeds.[184] In July 2024, it was reported that the company was laying off its diversity, equity, and</div>
<div id="LC655" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">inclusion (DEI) team.[185][186]</div>
<div id="LC656" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC657" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 19, 2024, a global IT outage impacted Microsoft services, affecting businesses, airlines, and</div>
<div id="LC658" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">financial institutions worldwide. The outage was traced back to a flawed update of CrowdStrike&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC659" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cybersecurity software, which resulted in Microsoft systems crashing and causing disruptions across</div>
<div id="LC660" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">various sectors. Despite CrowdStrike&#39;s CEO George Kurtz clarifying that the issue was not a cyberattack,</div>
<div id="LC661" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the incident had widespread consequences, leading to delays in air travel, financial transactions, and</div>
<div id="LC662" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">medical services globally. Microsoft stated that the underlying cause had been fixed but acknowledged</div>
<div id="LC663" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">ongoing residual impacts on some Microsoft 365 apps and services.[187][188]</div>
<div id="LC664" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC665" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In September 2024, BlackRock and Microsoft announced a $30 billion fund, the Global AI Infrastructure</div>
<div id="LC666" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Investment Partnership, to invest in AI infrastructure such as data centers and energy projects. The fund</div>
<div id="LC667" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">has the potential to reach $100 billion with debt financing, and partners include Abu Dhabi-backed MGX</div>
<div id="LC668" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and Nvidia, which will provide AI expertise. Investments will primarily focus on the U.S., with some in</div>
<div id="LC669" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">partner countries.[189] Microsoft also announced relaunch of its controversial tool, Recall, in November</div>
<div id="LC670" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2024 after addressing privacy concerns. Initially criticized for taking regular screenshots without user</div>
<div id="LC671" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">consent, Recall was changed to an opt-in feature instead of being default on. The UK&#39;s Information</div>
<div id="LC672" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Commissioner&#39;s Office monitored the situation and noted the adjustments, which included enhanced</div>
<div id="LC673" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">security measures like encryption and biometric access. While experts regarded these changes as</div>
<div id="LC674" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">improvements, they advised caution, with some recommending further testing before users opted in.[190]</div>
<div id="LC675" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC676" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On February 28, 2025, Microsoft announced that Skype would be shutting down on May 5, 2025, to</div>
<div id="LC677" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">streamline its focus on Microsoft Teams. The company stated there would be no job cuts due to the</div>
<div id="LC678" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">shutdown.[191]</div>
<div id="LC679" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC680" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On April 4, 2025, it celebrated its 50th anniversary.[192]</div>
<div id="LC681" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC682" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On May 30, 2025, it was reported that Microsoft&#39;s Russian division would be preparing to file for</div>
<div id="LC683" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">bankruptcy, days after President Vladimir Putin stated that foreign services providers should be throttled</div>
<div id="LC684" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in Russia to make way for domestic software, which included Microsoft. The company previously</div>
<div id="LC685" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">restructured operations in Russia in June 2022 after being significantly impacted by the Russian invasion</div>
<div id="LC686" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of Ukraine, but stated those restructuring efforts have failed.[193]</div>
<div id="LC687" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC688" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On July 2, 2025, Microsoft announced to cut nearly 4% of its workforce, around 9,000 jobs, to control</div>
<div id="LC689" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">costs amid heavy AI infrastructure spending, while also restructuring management and streamlining</div>
<div id="LC690" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">operations.[194]</div>
<div id="LC691" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC692" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Corporate affairs</div>
<div id="LC693" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft is ranked No. 14 in the 2022 Fortune 500 rankings of the largest United States corporations by</div>
<div id="LC694" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">total revenue;[195] and it was the world&#39;s largest software maker by revenue in 2022 according to Forbes</div>
<div id="LC695" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Global 2000. In 2018, Microsoft became the most valuable publicly traded company in the world,[196] a</div>
<div id="LC696" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC697" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC698" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC699" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">position it has repeatedly traded with Apple in the years since.[197] In April 2019, Microsoft reached a</div>
<div id="LC700" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">trillion-dollar market cap, becoming the third U.S. public company to be valued at over $1 trillion.[d] As</div>
<div id="LC701" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of 2024, Microsoft has the third-highest global brand valuation. Microsoft is one of only two U.S.-based</div>
<div id="LC702" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">companies that have a prime credit rating of AAA.[198]</div>
<div id="LC703" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC704" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Board of directors</div>
<div id="LC705" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">The company is run by a board of directors made up of mostly company outsiders, as is customary for</div>
<div id="LC706" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">publicly traded companies. Members of the board of directors as of December 2023 are Satya Nadella,</div>
<div id="LC707" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Reid Hoffman, Hugh Johnston, Teri List, Sandi Peterson, Penny Pritzker, Carlos Rodriguez, Charles</div>
<div id="LC708" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Scharf, John W. Stanton, John W. Thompson, Emma Walmsley and Padmasree Warrior.[199]</div>
<div id="LC709" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC710" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Board members are elected every year at the annual shareholders&#39; meeting using a majority vote system.</div>
<div id="LC711" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">There are four committees within the board that oversee more specific matters. These committees include</div>
<div id="LC712" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Audit Committee, which handles accounting issues with the company including auditing and</div>
<div id="LC713" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">reporting; the Compensation Committee, which approves compensation for the CEO and other employees</div>
<div id="LC714" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of the company; the Governance and Nominating Committee, which handles various corporate matters</div>
<div id="LC715" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">including the nomination of the board; and the Regulatory and Public Policy Committee, which includes</div>
<div id="LC716" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">legal/antitrust matters, along with privacy, trade, digital safety, artificial intelligence, and environmental</div>
<div id="LC717" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">sustainability.[200]</div>
<div id="LC718" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC719" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On March 13, 2020, Gates announced that he is leaving the board of directors of Microsoft and Berkshire</div>
<div id="LC720" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Hathaway to focus more on his philanthropic efforts. According to Aaron Tilley of The Wall Street</div>
<div id="LC721" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Journal this is &quot;marking the biggest boardroom departure in the tech industry since the death of longtime</div>
<div id="LC722" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">rival and Apple Inc. co-founder Steve Jobs.&quot;[201]</div>
<div id="LC723" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC724" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On January 13, 2022, The Wall Street Journal reported that Microsoft&#39;s board of directors plans to hire an</div>
<div id="LC725" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">external law firm to review its sexual harassment and gender discrimination policies, and to release a</div>
<div id="LC726" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">summary of how the company handled past allegations of misconduct against Bill Gates and other</div>
<div id="LC727" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">corporate executives.[202]</div>
<div id="LC728" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC729" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Chief executives</div>
<div id="LC730" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">1. Bill Gates (1975–2000)</div>
<div id="LC731" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2. Steve Ballmer (2000–2014)</div>
<div id="LC732" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">3. Satya Nadella (2014–present)</div>
<div id="LC733" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC734" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Financial</div>
<div id="LC735" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">When Microsoft went public and launched its initial public offering (IPO) in 1986, the opening stock</div>
<div id="LC736" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">price was $21; after the trading day, the price closed at $27.75. As of July 2010, with the company&#39;s nine</div>
<div id="LC737" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">stock splits, any IPO shares would be multiplied by 288; if one were to buy the IPO today, given the splits</div>
<div id="LC738" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and other factors, it would cost about 9  cents.[10]: 235–236 [204][205] The stock price peaked in 1999 at</div>
<div id="LC739" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">around $119 ($60.928, adjusting for splits).[206] The company began to offer a dividend on January 16,</div>
<div id="LC740" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2003, starting at eight cents per share for the fiscal year followed by a dividend of sixteen cents per share</div>
<div id="LC741" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the subsequent year, switching from yearly to quarterly dividends in 2005 with eight cents a share per</div>
<div id="LC742" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC743" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC744" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC745" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">quarter and a special one-time payout</div>
<div id="LC746" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of three dollars per share for the</div>
<div id="LC747" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">second quarter of the fiscal</div>
<div id="LC748" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">year.[206][207] Though the company</div>
<div id="LC749" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">had subsequent increases in dividend</div>
<div id="LC750" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">payouts, the price of Microsoft&#39;s stock</div>
<div id="LC751" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">remained steady for years.[207][208]</div>
<div id="LC752" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC753" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Standard &amp; Poor&#39;s and Moody&#39;s</div>
<div id="LC754" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Investors Service have both given a</div>
<div id="LC755" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">AAA rating to Microsoft, whose Five year history graph of Nasdaq: MSFT (https://www.nasdaq.co</div>
<div id="LC756" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">assets were valued at $41  billion as m/market-activity/stocks/msft) stock on July 17, 2013.[203]</div>
<div id="LC757" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC758" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">compared to only $8.5  billion in</div>
<div id="LC759" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">unsecured debt. Consequently, in</div>
<div id="LC760" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">February 2011 Microsoft released a corporate bond amounting to $2.25  billion with relatively low</div>
<div id="LC761" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">borrowing rates compared to government bonds.[209] For the first time in 20 years Apple Inc. surpassed</div>
<div id="LC762" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft in Q1 2011 quarterly profits and revenues due to a slowdown in PC sales and continuing huge</div>
<div id="LC763" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">losses in Microsoft&#39;s Online Services Division (which contains its search engine Bing). Microsoft profits</div>
<div id="LC764" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">were $5.2 billion, while Apple Inc. profits were $6 billion, on revenues of $14.5 billion and $24.7 billion</div>
<div id="LC765" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">respectively.[210] Microsoft&#39;s Online Services Division has been continuously loss-making since 2006 and</div>
<div id="LC766" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in Q1 2011 it lost $726 million. This follows a loss of $2.5 billion for the year 2010.[211]</div>
<div id="LC767" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">

<div id="LC775" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">As of January 2014, Microsoft&#39;s market capitalization stood</div>

<div id="LC833" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In November 2018, the company won a $480 million military contract with the U.S. government to bring</div>
<div id="LC834" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">augmented reality (AR) headset technology into the weapon repertoires of American soldiers. The two-</div>
<div id="LC835" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">year contract may result in follow-on orders of more than 100,000 headsets, according to documentation</div>
<div id="LC836" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">describing the bidding process. One of the contract&#39;s tag lines for the augmented reality technology seems</div>
<div id="LC837" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">to be its ability to enable &quot;25 bloodless battles before the 1st battle&quot;, suggesting that actual combat</div>
<div id="LC838" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">training is going to be an essential aspect of the augmented reality headset capabilities.[222]</div>

<div id="LC840" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Subsidiaries</div>
<div id="LC841" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft is an international business. As such, it needs subsidiaries present in whatever national markets</div>
<div id="LC842" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">it chooses to harvest. An example is Microsoft Canada, which it established in 1985.[223] Other countries</div>
<div id="LC843" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">have similar installations, to funnel profits back up to Redmond and to distribute the dividends to the</div>
<div id="LC844" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">holders of MSFT stock.</div>
