<div id="LC878" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2004, Microsoft commissioned research firms to do</div>
<div id="LC879" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">independent studies comparing the total cost of ownership</div>
<div id="LC880" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(TCO) of Windows Server 2003 to Linux; the firms</div>
<div id="LC881" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">concluded that companies found Windows easier to</div>
<div id="LC882" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">administrate than Linux, thus those using Windows would</div>
<div id="LC883" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">administrate faster resulting in lower costs for their company</div>
<div id="LC884" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">(i.e. lower TCO).[225] This spurred a wave of related studies;</div>
<div id="LC885" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">a study by the Yankee Group concluded that upgrading from</div>
<div id="LC886" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">one version of Windows Server to another costs a fraction of</div>
<div id="LC887" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the switching costs from Windows Server to Linux, although Windows 8 Launch Event in Akihabara,</div>
<div id="LC888" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">companies surveyed noted the increased security and Tokyo, on October 25, 2012</div>
<div id="LC889" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">reliability of Linux servers and concern about being locked</div>
<div id="LC890" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">into using Microsoft products.[226] Another study, released by</div>
<div id="LC891" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">the Open Source Development Labs, claimed that the Microsoft studies were &quot;simply outdated and one-</div>
<div id="LC892" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">sided&quot; and their survey concluded that the TCO of Linux was lower due to Linux administrators</div>
<div id="LC893" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">managing more servers on average and other reasons.[227]</div>
<div id="LC894" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC895" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">As part of the &quot;Get the Facts&quot; campaign, Microsoft highlighted the .NET Framework trading platform</div>
<div id="LC896" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">that it had developed in partnership with Accenture for the London Stock Exchange, claiming that it</div>
<div id="LC897" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">provided &quot;five nines&quot; reliability. After suffering extended downtime and unreliability[228][229] the London</div>
<div id="LC898" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Stock Exchange announced in 2009 that it was planning to drop its Microsoft solution and switch to a</div>
<div id="LC899" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Linux-based one in 2010.[230][231]</div>
<div id="LC900" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC901" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2012, Microsoft hired a political pollster named Mark Penn, whom The New York Times called</div>
<div id="LC902" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;famous for bulldozing&quot; his political opponents[232] as Executive Vice-president, Advertising and</div>
<div id="LC903" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Strategy. Penn created a series of negative advertisements targeting one of Microsoft&#39;s chief competitors,</div>
<div id="LC904" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Google. The advertisements, called &quot;Scroogled&quot;, attempted to make the case that Google is &quot;screwing&quot;</div>
<div id="LC905" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">consumers with search results rigged to favor Google&#39;s paid advertisers, that Gmail violates the privacy of</div>
<div id="LC906" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC907" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC908" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC909" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">its users to place ad results related to the content of their emails and shopping results, which favor Google</div>
<div id="LC910" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">products. Tech publications like TechCrunch were highly critical of the advertising campaign,[233] while</div>
<div id="LC911" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Google employees embraced it.[234]</div>
<div id="LC912" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC913" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Layoffs</div>
<div id="LC914" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In July 2014, Microsoft announced plans to lay off 18,000 employees. Microsoft employed 127,104</div>
<div id="LC915" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">people as of June 5, 2014, making this about a 14 percent reduction of its workforce as the biggest</div>
<div id="LC916" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft layoff ever. This included 12,500 professional and factory personnel. Previously, Microsoft had</div>
<div id="LC917" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">eliminated 5,800 jobs in 2009 in line with the Great Recession of 2008–2017.[235][236] In September</div>
<div id="LC918" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2014, Microsoft laid off 2,100 people, including 747 people in the Seattle–Redmond area, where the</div>
<div id="LC919" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company is headquartered. The firings came as a second wave of the layoffs that were previously</div>
<div id="LC920" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">announced. This brought the total number to over 15,000 out of the 18,000 expected cuts.[237] In October</div>
<div id="LC921" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2014, Microsoft revealed that it was almost done with eliminating 18,000 employees, which was its</div>
<div id="LC922" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">largest-ever layoff sweep.[238] In July 2015, Microsoft announced another 7,800 job cuts in the next</div>
<div id="LC923" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">several months.[239] In May 2016, Microsoft announced another 1,850 job cuts mostly in its Nokia</div>
<div id="LC924" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">mobile phone division. As a result, the company will record an impairment and restructuring charge of</div>
<div id="LC925" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">approximately $950 million, of which approximately $200 million will relate to severance payments.[240]</div>
<div id="LC926" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC927" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft laid off 1,900 employees in its gaming division in January 2024. The layoffs primarily affected</div>
<div id="LC928" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Activision Blizzard employees, but some Xbox and ZeniMax employees were also affected.[241] Blizzard</div>
<div id="LC929" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">president Mike Ybarra and chief design officer Allen Adham also resigned.[242]</div>
<div id="LC930" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC931" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In May 2025, Microsoft announced that it is laying off more than 6,000 employees, around three percent</div>
<div id="LC932" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">of the company&#39;s entire workforce.[243] In July 2025, Microsoft announced another round of layoffs,</div>
<div id="LC933" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cutting approximately 9,000 employees in its largest workforce reduction in over two years. The cuts</div>
<div id="LC934" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">affected multiple divisions, including Xbox, with 830 positions eliminated at its Redmond, Washington</div>
<div id="LC935" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">headquarters.[244][245] Amid the layoffs, Microsoft also announced that it was quitting Pakistan after 25</div>
<div id="LC936" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">years of operations.[246][247] While the exit was part of shift in business strategies and preference for</div>
<div id="LC937" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">cloud based models, the company&#39;s exit from Pakistan was also reportedly attributed to &quot;growing</div>
<div id="LC938" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">difficulties&quot; with the country&#39;s business environment.[248]</div>
<div id="LC939" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC940" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Unions</div>
<div id="LC941" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft recognizes seven trade unions[e] representing 1,750 workers in the United States at its video</div>
<div id="LC942" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">game subsidiaries Activision Blizzard and ZeniMax Media.[249] U.S. workers have been vocal in</div>
<div id="LC943" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">opposing military and law-enforcement contracts with Microsoft.[250] Bethesda Game Studios is</div>
<div id="LC944" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">unionized in Canada.[251] Microsoft South Korea has recognized its union since 2017.[252][253] German</div>
<div id="LC945" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">employees have elected works councils since 1998.[254]</div>
<div id="LC946" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC947" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">United States government</div>
<div id="LC948" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft provides information about reported bugs in its software to intelligence agencies of the United</div>
<div id="LC949" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">States government, prior to the public release of the fix. A Microsoft spokesperson stated that the</div>
<div id="LC950" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">corporation runs several programs that facilitate the sharing of such information with the U.S.</div>
<div id="LC951" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">government.[255] Following media reports about PRISM, NSA&#39;s massive electronic surveillance program,</div>
<div id="LC952" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC953" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC954" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC955" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">in May 2013, several technology companies were identified as participants, including Microsoft.[256]</div>
<div id="LC956" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC957" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">According to leaks of said program, Microsoft joined the PRISM program in 2007.[257] However, in June</div>
<div id="LC958" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">2013, an official statement from Microsoft flatly denied its participation in the program:</div>
<div id="LC959" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC960" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">&quot;We provide customer data only when we receive a legally binding order or subpoena to do so,</div>
<div id="LC961" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and never on a voluntary basis. In addition, we only ever comply with orders for requests about</div>
<div id="LC962" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">specific accounts or identifiers. If the government has a broader voluntary national security</div>
<div id="LC963" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">program to gather customer data, we don&#39;t participate in it.&quot;[258]</div>
<div id="LC964" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC965" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">During the first six months of 2013, Microsoft received requests that affected between 15,000 and 15,999</div>
<div id="LC966" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">accounts.[259] In December 2013, the company made a statement to further emphasize that it takes its</div>
<div id="LC967" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">customers&#39; privacy and data protection very seriously, saying that &quot;government snooping potentially now</div>
<div id="LC968" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">constitutes an &#39;advanced persistent threat,&#39; alongside sophisticated malware and cyber attacks&quot;.[260] The</div>
<div id="LC969" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">statement also marked the beginning of three-part program to enhance Microsoft&#39;s encryption and</div>
<div id="LC970" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">transparency efforts. On July 1, 2014, as part of this program, it opened the first (of many) Microsoft</div>
<div id="LC971" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Transparency Center, which provides &quot;participating governments with the ability to review source code</div>
<div id="LC972" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">for our key products, assure themselves of their software integrity, and confirm there are no &quot;back</div>
<div id="LC973" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">doors.&quot;[261] Microsoft has also argued that the United States Congress should enact strong privacy</div>
<div id="LC974" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">regulations to protect consumer data.[262]</div>
<div id="LC975" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC976" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In April 2016, the company sued the U.S. government, argued that secrecy orders were preventing the</div>
<div id="LC977" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">company from disclosing warrants to customers in violation of the company&#39;s and customers&#39; rights.</div>
<div id="LC978" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Microsoft argued that it was unconstitutional for the government to indefinitely ban Microsoft from</div>
<div id="LC979" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">informing its users that the government was requesting their emails and other documents and that the</div>
<div id="LC980" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Fourth Amendment made it so people or businesses had the right to know if the government searches or</div>
<div id="LC981" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">seizes their property. On October 23, 2017, Microsoft said it would drop the lawsuit as a result of a policy</div>
<div id="LC982" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">change by the United States Department of Justice (DoJ). The DoJ had &quot;changed data request rules on</div>
<div id="LC983" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">alerting the Internet users about agencies accessing their information.&quot;</div>
<div id="LC984" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC985" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">In 2022 Microsoft shared a $9 billion contract from the United States Department of Defense for cloud</div>
<div id="LC986" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">computing with Amazon, Google, and Oracle.[263]</div>
<div id="LC987" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC988" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Security challenges</div>
<div id="LC989" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">On a Friday afternoon in January 2024, Microsoft disclosed that a Russian state-sponsored group hacked</div>
<div id="LC990" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">into its corporate systems. The group, accessed &quot;a very small percentage&quot; of Microsoft corporate email</div>
<div id="LC991" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">accounts, which also included members of its senior leadership team and employees in its cybersecurity</div>
<div id="LC992" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">and legal teams.[264] Microsoft noted in a blog post that the attack might have been prevented if the</div>
<div id="LC993" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">accounts in question had enabled multi-factor authentication, a defensive measure which is widely</div>
<div id="LC994" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">recommended in the industry, including by Microsoft itself.[265]</div>
<div id="LC995" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC996" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC997" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC998" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Corporate identity</div>
<div id="LC999" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">
</div>
<div id="LC1000" class="react-code-text react-code-line-contents-no-virtualization react-file-line html-div ">Corporate culture</div>
